=== BUGGY CODE ===
class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for i, j in edges:
            g[i].append(j)
            g[j].append(i)
            
        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n
        
        def dfs(i, l, p:
            level[i] = l
            parent[i] = p
            for j in g[i]:
                if j != p:
                    dfs(j, l + 1, i)
        
        def LCA(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a
        
        dfs(0, 0, -1)
        for i, j in trips:
            lca = LCA(i, j)
            path = []
            while i != lca:
                freq[i] += 1
                i = parent[i]
            freq[i] += 1
            while j != lca:
                freq[j] += 1
                j = parent[j]
        
        def dp(i, p):
            res0 = 0
            res1 = price[i] // 2 * freq[i]
            for j in g[i]:
                if j != p:
                    curr = dp(j, i)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]
        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))

Observe the following faulty python code. It contains one or more bugs.
Fix the code and output only the fixed code.

```python
class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for i, j in edges:
            g[i].append(j)
            g[j].append(i)
            
        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n
        
        def dfs(i, l, p:
            level[i] = l
            parent[i] = p
            for j in g[i]:
                if j != p:
                    dfs(j, l + 1, i)
        
        def LCA(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a
        
        dfs(0, 0, -1)
        for i, j in trips:
            lca = LCA(i, j)
            path = []
            while i != lca:
                freq[i] += 1
                i = parent[i]
            freq[i] += 1
            while j != lca:
                freq[j] += 1
                j = parent[j]
        
        def dp(i, p):
            res0 = 0
            res1 = price[i] // 2 * freq[i]
            for j in g[i]:
                if j != p:
                    curr = dp(j, i)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]
        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))
```

Reason in a repair-and-recheck way.

A practical pattern for this task usually looks like this:
- Start from the clearest failing symptom, suspicious line, or local inconsistency.
- Build the smallest explanation that accounts for that symptom, then check it against the nearby control flow, state updates, and dependent conditions.
- Once one repair path is reasonably supported, move into corrected code early and prefer a fix that can be checked against the same failing path.
- After the edit, rerun that path or the most relevant edge case.
- Then do one small coupled-region scan: a directly connected guard, index update, return path, or call site that could still break the same fix.
- If the recheck is still off, refine the same local region before opening a distant new theory.
- Once the failing path and its directly coupled region look consistent, finalize the code.

Try to avoid patterns like these:
- staying in diagnosis mode for too long without testing the next plausible edit;
- moving from a local repair into a fresh branch of speculative causes when the current path has not been rechecked yet;
- turning a focused fix into a broad rewrite unless the surrounding code clearly forces it.

Let's search memory of LC 2646 official solution. I remember code:
```
class Solution {
public:
    int minimumTotalPrice(int n, vector<vector<int>>& edges, vector<int>& price, vector<vector<int>>& trips) {
        vector<vector<int>> g(n);
        for (auto &e: edges) { ... }
        vector<int> cnt(n);
        for (auto &t: trips) {
            // find path via dfs
        }
        function<pair<int,int>(int,int)> dfs = [&](int u, int fa) -> pair<int,int> {
            int a = price[u] * cnt[u] / 2;
            int b = price[u] * cnt[u];
            for (int v: g[u]) {
                if (v == fa) continue;
                auto [x,y] = dfs(v,u);
                a += y;
                b += min(x,y);
            }
            return {a,b};
        };
        auto [x,y] = dfs(0,-1);
        return min(x,y);
    }
};
```
Yes, they use `price[u] * cnt[u] / 2`, not `(price[u]/2)*cnt[u]`. This is important. But is that mathematically "reduce price by 50%"? So official formula `price*cnt/2` with integer division gives floor of exact total. For cnt=1, exact 1.5, floor 1. But if price is odd and cnt odd, exact .5, floor loses .5. LeetCode return int, so they must floor. But if they instead define selected price as `price[i] // 2` per node, then for cnt=2 cost=2, which is not exact half. Which is more plausible?
```
class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for i, j in edges:
            g[i].append(j)
            g[j].append(i)
            
        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n
        
        def dfs(i, l, p:
            level[i] = l
            parent[i] = p
            for j in g[i]:
                if j != p:
                    dfs(j, l + 1, i)
        
        def LCA(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a
        
        dfs(0, 0, -1)
        for i, j in trips:
            lca = LCA(i, j)
            path = []
            while i != lca:
                freq[i] += 1
                i = parent[i]
            freq[i] += 1
            while j != lca:
                freq[j] += 1
                j = parent[j]
        
        def dp(i, p):
            res0 = 0
            res1 = price[i] // 2 * freq[i]
            for j in g[i]:
                if j != p:
                    curr = dp(j, i)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]
        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))
```

```
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for a,b in edges:
```

Now, what exactly should final code look like? We need output only fixed code. We can choose to rewrite the DP to cost direct, which is clearer and fixes the savings formula. Also fix syntax and maybe remove unused `path`. Need ensure code remains minimal.

Let's produce fixed code:

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(i: int, l: int, p: int) -> None:
            level[i] = l
            parent[i] = p
            for j in g[i]:
                if j != p:
                    dfs(j, l + 1, i)

        def lca(a: int, b: int) -> int:
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            w = lca(a, b)
            x, y = a, b
            while x != w:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[x] += 1
            while y != w:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(i: int, p: int):
            not_halved = price[i] * freq[i]
            halved = price[i] * freq[i] // 2
            for j in g[i]:
                if j != p:
                    child_not, child_halved = dp(j, i)
                    not_halved += min(child_not, child_halved)
                    halved += child_not
            return [not_halved, halved]

        return min(dp(0, -1))
```

```
class Solution {
public:
    int minimumTotalPrice(int n, vector<vector<int>>& edges, vector<int>& price, vector<vector<int>>& trips) {
        vector<vector<int>> g(n);
        for (auto &e : edges) {
            g[e[0]].push_back(e[1]);
            g[e[1]].push_back(e[0]);
        }
        vector<int> cnt(n);
        for (auto &t : trips) {
            // dfs to find path
        }
        function<pair<int,int>(int,int)> dfs = [&](int u, int fa) -> pair<int,int> {
            pair<int,int> res = {price[u] * cnt[u] / 2, price[u] * cnt[u]};
            for (int v : g[u]) {
                if (v == fa) continue;
                auto [x, y] = dfs(v, u);
                res.first += y;
                res.second += min(x, y);
            }
            return res;
        };
        auto res = dfs(0, -1);
        return min(res.first, res.second);
    }
};
```

Let's write final code:

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(i: int, l: int, p: int) -> None:
            level[i] = l
            parent[i] = p
            for j in g[i]:
                if j != p:
                    dfs(j, l + 1, i)

        def lca(a: int, b: int) -> int:
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            w = lca(a, b)
            x, y = a, b
            while x != w:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[x] += 1
            while y != w:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(i: int, p: int):
            not_halved = price[i] * freq[i]
            halved = price[i] * freq[i] // 2
            for j in g[i]:
                if j != p:
                    child_not, child_halved = dp(j, i)
                    not_halved += min(child_not, child_halved)
                    halved += child_not
            return [not_halved, halved]

        return min(dp(0, -1))
```

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(i: int, l: int, p: int) -> None:
            level[i] = l
            parent[i] = p
            for j in g[i]:
                if j != p:
                    dfs(j, l + 1, i)

        def LCA(a: int, b: int) -> int:
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            w = LCA(a, b)
            x, y = a, b
            while x != w:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[x] += 1
            while y != w:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(i: int, p: int):
            not_halved = price[i] * freq[i]
            halved = price[i] * freq[i] // 2
            for j in g[i]:
                if j != p:
                    child_not, child_halved = dp(j, i)
                    not_halved += min(child_not, child_halved)
                    halved += child_not
            return [not_halved, halved]

        return min(dp(0, -1))
```
Syntax okay.

Good. a=1,b=2 in tree 0-1-2: level1=1, level2=2, d=1, b=parent[2]=1, a==b return1. Good. a=2,b=3 in tree 0-1-2 and 0-1-3? level2=2, level3=2, d=0, while a!=b: a=parent[2]=1, b=parent[3]=1, return1. Now, let's consider if the original code's `res0 += max(curr)` and `res1 += curr[0]` with savings DP is equivalent to cost DP with `not_halved` and `halved`? Let's derive: full cost = ans. Savings if node not halved = full_subtree - cost_not; if node halved = full_subtree - cost_halved. The DP savings recurrence:
- res0 (savings if node not halved) = 0 + sum(max(child savings)) because if node not halved, each child can choose max savings. Cost_not = full_subtree - res0.
- res1 (savings if node halved) = (full_node - halved_node) + sum(child_not_savings) because children cannot halve. Cost_halved = full_subtree - res1.
Then ans - max(res) = min(cost). So equivalent. Good.

=== RAW ANSWER ===
```python
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(i: int, l: int, p: int) -> None:
            level[i] = l
            parent[i] = p
            for j in g[i]:
                if j != p:
                    dfs(j, l + 1, i)

        def LCA(a: int, b: int) -> int:
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            w = LCA(a, b)
            x, y = a, b
            while x != w:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[x] += 1
            while y != w:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(i: int, p: int):
            not_halved = price[i] * freq[i]
            halved = price[i] * freq[i] // 2

            for j in g[i]:
                if j != p:
                    child_not, child_halved = dp(j, i)
                    not_halved += min(child_not, child_halved)
                    halved += child_not

            return [not_halved, halved]

        return min(dp(0, -1))
```

=== FIXED CODE ===
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(i: int, l: int, p: int) -> None:
            level[i] = l
            parent[i] = p
            for j in g[i]:
                if j != p:
                    dfs(j, l + 1, i)

        def LCA(a: int, b: int) -> int:
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            w = LCA(a, b)
            x, y = a, b
            while x != w:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[x] += 1
            while y != w:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(i: int, p: int):
            not_halved = price[i] * freq[i]
            halved = price[i] * freq[i] // 2

            for j in g[i]:
                if j != p:
                    child_not, child_halved = dp(j, i)
                    not_halved += min(child_not, child_halved)
                    halved += child_not

            return [not_halved, halved]

        return min(dp(0, -1))
